Let's write recurrence carefully:
def sqrt_series(c, n):
    # c length >= n
    if c[0] < 0? return None
    a = np.empty(n)
    inv2a0 = 1/(2*sqrt(c0))
    a[0]=sqrt(c0)
    # need for k 1..n-1: sum_{i=1}^{k-1} a_i a_{k-i}
    # We can update convolution of a with itself incrementally? Direct:
    for k in range(1,n):
       # For k=1 sum=0
       # We can compute using np.convolve(a[:k], a[:k])? That gives full up to 2k-1; too expensive if each.
       # Use dot: if k>1: s=np.dot(a[1:k], a[k-1:0:-1])
       # But a[k] not set yet; ok.
       a[k]=(c[k]-s)*inv2a0
    return a
Total multiplications ∑(k-1) ~ n^2/2. For n=12288=75M. Each numpy dot in C; but 12288 Python calls. Could be maybe 1s per 10M? 7.5s? Let's estimate: 75M float64 multiply-add ~0.15 GFLOP? Actually 75M is 0.15e9; numpy dot BLAS maybe 10 GFLOP/s? But small vectors many calls overhead; maybe 0.5-2s. Acceptable for 10 sqrt (10-20s). For 100 sqrt maybe 200s. Good. We can cache? `np.dot` for length 1..12287; small dot overhead significant but okay. Could use `np.convolve` on whole a? Not incremental. Could implement in blocks with FFT to reduce to O(n log n) but overkill.

However recurrence with direct dot assumes c has coefficient c[k] provided. If c[k] is capped length 2n-1, for k up to n-1. Good. If c[0] zero, problem. We can ensure c0>0 by adding floor; if not, fail.

We can write project_box_sum efficient O(N) using sort or direct. Since M fixed, to project a sum1 vector C:
- x = np.minimum(C, M)
- deficit = 1 - x.sum()
- if deficit <= tol: return x (if maybe slight negative)
- mask = x < M (strict)
- slack = M - x[mask]
- If slack sum <= 0 or deficit <=0: if deficit >0 and slack small, set x[mask]=M? But if M<1/N, infeasible; assume M≥1/N.
- x[mask] += deficit * slack / slack.sum()
- return x
This yields all x≤M and sum1. But adding proportional to slack may make some exactly M, fine. It's not exact Euclidean projection if adding equally after filling? Actually if deficit/avg slack could exceed some slack? We scale by slack so all below M remain below (weighted), no violation. It preserves the shape of slack. Good.

What about lower bound 0? C nonnegative; x nonnegative. Good.

def _cspace_feasible(a, M, max_iter):
    C=conv(a)
    for it in range(max_iter):
       C=project_box_sum(C,M)
       a_new=sqrt_series(C,n)
       a_new=clip,normalize
       C_new=conv(a_new)
       if max(C_new) <= M+tol: return a_new, True
       C = C_new  # or blend with C_proj?
    return None, False
```

def _poly_mul_trunc(a,b,m):
    if m<=0 return []
    la=min(len(a),m); lb=min(len(b),m)
    if la==0 or lb==0 return np.zeros(m)
    out_len=la+lb-1

FFT convolution:
```
def _next_pow2(n):
    x=1
    while x<n: x*=2
    return x

def _convolve_fast(a,b):
    n=len(a); m=len(b); out=n+m-1
    N=_next_pow2(out)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,N)[:out]

def _auto(a):
    n=len(a); out=2*n-1; N=_next_pow2(out)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:out]
```
For n up to maybe 32768, out ~65535, N=131072. FFT 32k length, 1 CPU okay. Each auto ~0.005-0.02s? Actually rfft 131k ~0.005, inverse ~0.005; fine. 1000 autos ~5-10s.

Score:
```
def _score(a):
    s=a.sum(); if s<=0 return inf
    c=_auto(a)
    return float(2*len(a)*c.max()/(s*s))
```
We can maintain sum normalized sum=1; then score=2n*max.

Normalize:
```
def _normalize(a):
    a=np.maximum(np.asarray(a,float),0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-15: return np.ones(len(a))/len(a)
    return a/s
```

def _project_box_sum(c, M):
    c=np.asarray(c,float)
    if M <= 0: return c
    # ensure sum? c may not sum exactly; first scale to 1? We expect sum1.
    x = np.minimum(c, M)
    deficit = 1.0 - x.sum()
    if deficit <= 1e-14:
        if deficit < -1e-12:
            # c sum not 1? normalize?
            pass
        return x
    mask = x < M
    if not mask.any():
        # cannot distribute; return x scaled? infeasible
        return x
    slack = M - x[mask]
    sl = slack.sum()
    if sl <= 1e-300:
        return x
    x = x.copy()
    x[mask] += deficit * (slack / sl)
    # numerical ensure no > M + small
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, M)
    x /= x.sum()  # This may break max if sum>1 slightly; if max clipped, scaling down okay; if max at M and scaling down <M.
    # But if scaling down after clip, max≤M. If scaling up? x sum likely 1.
    return x
```

Direct sqrt:
```
def _sqrt_direct(c,n):
    a=np.empty(n,float)
    a0=np.sqrt(c[0]); a[0]=a0; inv2=1/(2*a0)
    for k in range(1,n):
       if k==1:
          s=0.0
       else:
          # Use np.dot(a[1:k], a[k-1:0:-1])
          s=np.dot(a[1:k], a[k-1:0:-1])
       a[k]=(c[k]-s)*inv2
    return a
```
For negative strides, maybe use `a[k-1::-1][:k-1]`? `a[k-1:0:-1]` includes index k-1 down to1 length k-1, correct. For k=1 empty. For k=2, a[1:0:-1] length1 [a1] times a[1:0:-1]? Newton sqrt: Need handle c array length maybe N, but use only first n. Ensure c is at least n.
```
def _sqrt_newton(c, m):
    c0=c[0]
    if c0 <= 0: return None
    A=np.array([np.sqrt(c0)],float)
    l=1
    # pre-convert c_use? We'll slice each loop
    while l < m:
       nl = min(2*l, m)
       B = _poly_inv(A, nl)
       if B is None: return None
       CB = _poly_mul_trunc(c[:nl], B, nl)
       A_pad = np.zeros(nl)
       A_pad[:l] = A
       A = 0.5*(A_pad + CB)
       l = nl
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(A)): return None
    return A
```
But note: `_poly_inv(A, nl)` uses A length l; if A has length l, okay. However when l doubles, A is truncated to current. Good.

`_poly_inv` direct for m small? For m=2, etc. Use FFT threshold.
```
def _poly_inv(c, m):
    c0=c[0]
    if not finite or c0<=1e-300: return None
    B=np.array([1.0/c0])
    l=1
    while l<m:
       nl=min(2*l,m)
       c_use = np.asarray(c[:nl], float)
       CB = _poly_mul_trunc(c_use, B, nl)
       factor = -CB
       factor[0] += 2.0
       B = _poly_mul_trunc(B, factor, nl)
       l = nl
    return B
```

`_poly_mul_trunc` direct threshold:
```
def _poly_mul_trunc(a,b,m):
    if m <=0: return np.empty(0,float)
    la=min(len(a),m); lb=min(len(b),m)
    if la==0 or lb==0: return np.zeros(m,float)
    if la*lb <= 4096:
       r=np.convolve(a[:la], b[:lb])
       if len(r) >= m: return r[:m].astype(float)
       out=np.zeros(m); out[:len(r)]=r; return out
    out_len=la+lb-1
    N=_next_pow2(out_len)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a[:la], N)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(b[:lb], N)
    r=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb, N)
    if len(r)>=m: return r[:m]
    out=np.zeros(m); out[:len(r)]=r; return out
```
But if out_len < m, r shorter; pad zeros. If out_len > m, N >= out_len? We set nextpow2(out_len). To get first m without wrap, N>=out_len; good. If out_len much > m (e.g. la=lb=m, out_len=2m-1), N maybe 2^n. Could we reduce N to nextpow2(min(out_len, 2*m-1))? It is 2m-1. Good. If la=lb=m, out_len=2m-1. Fine. If la=m, lb=1, out_len=m, N=m; okay.

def _cspace_step(a, M, blend=0.2):
    n=len(a)
    C = _auto(a)
    C = C / C.sum() if sum not 1? a normalized sum1 so sum C=1.
    Cproj = _project_box_sum(C, M)
    # ensure c0 positive floor
    if Cproj[0] <= 1e-300: Cproj[0]=1e-300; adjust? maybe
    A = _cspace_sqrt(Cproj, n)
    if A is None: return None, Cproj, np.inf
    A = np.maximum(A, 0.0)
    s=A.sum()
    if not finite or s < 1e-12: return None, Cproj, np.inf
    A /= s
    Cnew = _auto(A)
    mnew = float(Cnew.max())
    Cnext = (1.0-blend)*Cnew + blend*Cproj
    Cnext = np.maximum(Cnext,0.0); ss=Cnext.sum(); if ss>0: Cnext /= ss
    return A, Cnext, mnew
```
But after normalize A, Cnew sum1. Good.

def _cspace_iter(C, M, blend):
    n = len? We don't know n from C (C length 2n-1). n=(len(C)+1)//2
    Cproj=_project_box_sum(C,M)
    # Ensure first coeff positive: if Cproj[0] < 1e-15, add floor? Use existing C[0] maybe.
    if Cproj[0] <= 1e-300:
        return None, None, np.inf
    A=_cspace_sqrt(Cproj, n)
    if A is None: return None,None,np.inf
    A=np.where(np.isfinite(A), A, 0.0)
    A=np.maximum(A,0.0)
    s=A.sum()
    if s < 1e-20: return None,None,np.inf
    A /= s
    Cnew=_auto_conv(A)
    mnew=float(Cnew.max())
    Cnext=(1.0-blend)*Cnew + blend*Cproj
    Cnext=np.maximum(Cnext,0.0)
    ss=Cnext.sum()
    if ss <= 1e-20: Cnext=Cproj
    else: Cnext/=ss
    return A, Cnext, mnew
```

`_project_box_sum` should take C sum1. If c maybe NaN, return c.
```
def _project_box_sum(c, M):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)): c=np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=M, neginf=0.0)
    s=c.sum(); if s<=0: return np.ones_like(c)/len(c)
    c=c/s
    if M <= 1.0/len(c): # infeasible? return flat?
       return np.ones_like(c)/len(c)
    x=np.minimum(c, M)
    deficit=1.0-x.sum()
    if deficit <= 1e-14:
       x = np.maximum(x,0.0)
       ss=x.sum(); if ss>0: x/=ss
       return x
    mask = x < M - 1e-15
    if not np.any(mask):
       return x/x.sum()
    slack = M - x[mask]
    sl = slack.sum()
    if sl <= 1e-300:
       return x
    x = x.copy()
    x[mask] += deficit * slack / sl
    # due to floating, some may exceed M by eps; clip
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, M)
    ss=x.sum()
    if ss <= 0: return c
    # if sum not 1, adjust a few below M
    diff=1.0-ss
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       free = x < M - 1e-12
       if np.any(free):
          x[free] += diff/np.count_nonzero(free)
          x=np.clip(x,0,M)
       else:
          x/=ss
    return x
```
If M very small and deficit negative? If sum cap >1, we need reduce? But M≥1/N and c sum1, sum min(c,M) ≤1 because if M≥avg, capped sum ≤1? If c sum1 and M≥1/N, min(c,M) sum ≤1 (deficit≥0). Good.

`_sqrt_direct` may be slow; for n <= 2048. Newton for bigger. Need handle `c` maybe list; convert.
```
def _sqrt_direct(c,n):
    c0=float(c[0])
    if c0 <= 0: return None
    a=np.empty(n,float); a0=np.sqrt(c0); a[0]=a0; inv=1/(2*a0)
    for k in range(1,n):
       if k>1:
          s=np.dot(a[1:k], a[k-1:0:-1])
       else: s=0.0
       a[k]=(float(c[k])-s)*inv
    return a
```
Potential Overflow if a0 tiny; check.

def _direction_eval(a, d, alpha, n):

    s=at.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.inf

def _golden_min(a,d,alpha_max,n,max_eval=20):
    if alpha_max <= 1e-15: return current?
    gr=(1+math.sqrt(5))/2
    a0=0.0; a1=alpha_max
    x1=a1 - (a1-a0)/gr
    x2=a0 + (a1-a0)/gr
    f1=_direction_eval(a,d,x1,n)
    if not finite: return inf,0,a
    f2=...
    best...

       if f1 < f2:
          a1=x2; x2=x1; f2=f1; x1=a1-(a1-a0)/gr
       else:
          a0=x1; x1=x2; f1=f2; x2=a0+(a1-a0)/gr
       evaluate new f1/f2 update best

def _active_polish(a, deadline, tol_start=0.004, tol_end=5e-6, blend? no, step_cap=0.5):
    n=len(a); a=np.maximum(a,0); a=a/a.sum(); best...
    tol=tol_start; no_improve=0; stall=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2:
       c=_auto_conv(a); m=float(c.max()); sc=2*n*m
       if sc<best_sc: update; no_improve=0; next=it? maybe compute active.
       # If tol too small and n_act 1? okay.
       Nlen=2*n-1
       beta_act = max(tol*m, m*1e-6)
       # weights only top active to avoid all? Use mask c >= m - tol*m
       active = c >= m - tol*m
       n_active=sum(active)
       if n_active < max(2, int(np.log2(n))): # top few
           idx=np.argpartition(c, -max(10,?))[-10:]; active=...
       elif n_active > max(50, n//100):
           # use top k = max(10, min(100, n//64))
           k=max(10, min(200, n//64))
           idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
           active=np.zeros_like(c,bool); active[idx]=True
       w=np.zeros(Nlen)
       vals=(c[active]-m)/beta_act
       ew=np.exp(np.clip(vals,-700,0))
       if ew.sum()==0: w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1
       else: w[active]=ew/ew.sum()
       ar=a[::-1]
       wc=_convolve_fast(w, ar)
       h=wc[n-1:2*n-1]
       grad=2.0*h
       grad_c=grad - grad.mean()
       d=-grad_c
       d_norm=np.linalg.norm(d)
       if d_norm < 1e-18: break
       neg=d<0
       if np.any(neg):
           alpha_max=np.min(a[neg]/(-d[neg]))
       else: alpha_max=np.inf
       if not np.isfinite(alpha_max): alpha_max=1.0
       # cap by norm to avoid huge? alpha_max=min(alpha_max*0.99, 0.5/(d_norm+1e-15), 0.5) maybe
       alpha_max=min(alpha_max*0.99, 0.5/(d_norm+1e-15), 0.5)
       if alpha_max <= 1e-15:
           # try tiny? break/reduce
       sc0=sc
       sc_new, alpha, a_new = _golden_min(a,d,alpha_max,n,max_eval=14)
       if sc_new < sc0 - 1e-14:
           a=a_new; sc_cur=sc_new
           if tol>tol_end: tol=max(tol_end, tol*0.99)
       else:
           # try top single peak direction?
           # If no improve, maybe reduce tol
           no_improve +=1
           if no_improve % 10 ==0:
              tol=max(tol_end, tol*0.6)
           if no_improve >= 80:
              a=best_a.copy()
              tol=max(tol_end, tol*0.5)
              no_improve=0
              stall +=1
              if stall>10: break
```

Let's write `_cspace_sqrt`:
```
def _cspace_sqrt(c,n):
    c=np.asarray(c,float)
    if c.size < n:
       tmp=np.zeros(n); tmp[:c.size]=c; c=tmp
    if n <= 128:
       A=_sqrt_direct(c,n)
    else:
       A=None
       try: A=_sqrt_newton(c,n)
       except: A=None
       if A is None or not finite or check:
          if n <= 4096: A=_sqrt_direct(c,n)
          else: return None
    return A
```
Check function:
```
def _sqrt_check(c,A,n):
    m=min(n,64)
    c0=A[0]
    for k in range(1,m):
       s=np.dot(A[1:k], A[k-1:0:-1]) if k>1 else 0.0
       if not isfinite(s): return False
       if abs(2*c0*A[k]+s - c[k]) > 1e-7*max(1.0, abs(c[k]), abs(2*c0*A[k])+abs(s)):
           return False
    return True
```
Tolerance 1e-7 maybe too strict for Newton roundoff over 16k? For first 64, errors small. Use 1e-5.

Now, C-space feasible with blend. If target success, return A; if not but best improved below initial, return best. It also can return `m_best` to caller? We'll just compute score.

```
def _cspace_feasible(a, M, max_iter, blend, deadline):
    n=len(a)
    a_best=a.copy(); sc_best=2*n*_auto_conv(a).max()
    C=_auto_conv(a_best)
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time>deadline: break
       A,Cnew,mnew=_cspace_iter(C,M,blend)
       if A is None: break
       scnew=2*n*mnew  # Cnew sum1? yes
       if scnew < sc_best:
          a_best=A.copy(); sc_best=scnew
       if mnew <= M*(1+1e-9)+1e-15:
          return A
       if Cnew is not None:
          # if Cnew almost unchanged, break
          if np.max(np.abs(Cnew-C)) < 1e-12*max(1.0,M):
              C=Cnew; break
          else:
              C=Cnew
       else:
          break
    return a_best
```
Note: `Cnew` sum1? After A normalize, auto sum=1. Good. `mnew` from Cnew.

If `mnew` is inf, break.

Now, verify LSE gradient formula with args. Scipy minimize with args: `fun(x, *args)`, `jac(x, *args)`. We'll define `_lse_fun(z, beta, n)`, `_lse_jac(z, beta, n)`.
Inside `_lse_jac`, if beta <=0? beta always >0.
```
def _lse_jac(z,beta,n):
    a=_softmax(z)
    c=_auto_conv(a)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    shifted=(c-cmax)/beta
    e=np.exp(np.clip(shifted,-700,0.0))
    s=e.sum()
    if s<1e-300:
       w=np.zeros_like(c); w[argmax(c)]=1
    else: w=e/s
    W=float(np.dot(w,c))
    ar=a[::-1]
    wc=_convolve_fast(w, ar)
    h=wc[n-1:2*n-1]
    grad=4.0*n*a*(h-W)
    return grad
```
This O(n log n) per jac. Good.

`_lse_fun` similar but returns scalar. Could avoid recompute if jac? minimize evaluates separately, no caching. Fine.

    return best_sc,0,a

def _cspace_shape_search(n, specs, deadline):
   best=None; best_sc=inf
   for name,p in specs:
      a=_profile(n,name,p)
      sc=_score(a)
      if sc<best_sc: ...
   if best is None: return ...
   # quick descent
   if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20:
      a_d, sc_d = _cspace_descent(best, min(deadline-20, time.monotonic()+60), max_iter=20 if n<=4096 else 10)
      update
   low = max(1.0001, best_sc*0.965)
   high = best_sc
   max_mids = 12
   for i in range(max_mids):
      if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
      if high - low < 1e-5: break
      mid=(low+high)/2
      M=mid/(2*n)
      # actual M lower bound
      A=_cspace_feasible(best, M, max_iter=18 if n<=4096 else 8, blend=0.15, deadline=deadline-3)
      scA=_score(A)
      if np.isfinite(scA):
         if scA <= mid*(1+1e-7):
             high=mid
             best=A.copy(); best_sc=scA
         else:
             low=mid
             if scA < best_sc:
                 best=A.copy(); best_sc=scA
   return best,best_sc
```

Let's write code now mentally. Include docstring at top.

```
"""
Search for nonnegative step-function heights minimizing 2n max(a*a)/(sum a)^2.
Combines C-space alternating projections (box on square coefficients + polynomial square root),
LSE L-BFGS in log-domain, and active-set peak shaving with sample line search.
"""
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize
```
Allowed imports. No evaluate_sequence import.

Functions:
```
def _next_pow2(n):
    if n <=1: return 1
    k=1
    while k<n: k <<=1
    return k
```

Conv:
```
def _convolve_fast(a,b):
    out_n=len(a)+len(b)-1
    if out_n <=0: return np.empty(0,float)
    N=_next_pow2(out_n)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,N)[:out_n]

def _auto_conv(a):
    N=_next_pow2(2*len(a)-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2*len(a)-1]
```
If a length 0? not.

Score:
```
def _score(a):
    if a is None: return np.inf
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.01: # but score invariant, if s small but sequence normalized? use 1e-15
       return np.inf
    c=_auto_conv(a)
    m=float(c.max())
    return float(2*len(a)*m/(s*s))
```
If returning sequence later normalized sum1, okay. In internal, if s<1e-15.

Normalize:
```
def _normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones_like(a)/len(a) if len(a)>0 else np.array([1.0])
    return a/s
```

Profile:
```
def _profile(n, kind='power', p=0.5, alpha=0.0, freq=2, amp=0.3, phase=0.0):
    if n<=0: return np.array([1.0])
    t=np.arange(n,float)
    tt=t/(max(n-1,1))
    if kind=='uniform': a=np.ones(n)
    elif kind=='power': a=(t+1.0+alpha)**(-float(p))
    elif kind=='power_rev': a=(n-t)**(-float(p)) # t from0 to n-1; n-t from n to1, positive
    elif kind=='exp': a=np.exp(-3.0*t/max(n,1))
    elif kind==='cos': a=1.0+amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*tt+phase)
    else: a=np.ones(n)
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-12)
    s=a.sum(); if s>0: a/=s
    return a
```
For power_rev, if t=n-1, n-t=1. Good.

Resample:
```
def _resample(a, new_n):
    old_n=len(a)
    if old_n==new_n: return _normalize(a)
    x0=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,old_n)
    x1=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
    b=np.interp(x1,x0,a)
    return _normalize(b)
```

Project box:
as above.

Sqrt direct/newton.
Need in direct, if c contains nan, replace? `_cspace_sqrt` can `np.nan_to_num`.

```
def _sqrt_direct(c,n):
    c0=float(c[0])
    if not math.isfinite(c0) or c0 <= 1e-300: return None
    a=np.empty(n,float)
    a0=math.sqrt(c0); a[0]=a0; inv=1.0/(2.0*a0)
    for k in range(1,n):
       if k >1:
          s=float(np.dot(a[1:k], a[k-1:0:-1]))
       else: s=0.0
       if not math.isfinite(s): # fallback? set using c
          s=0.0
       a[k]=(float(c[k])-s)*inv
    return a
```

Poly mul:
```
def _poly_mul_trunc(a,b,m):
    if m <=0: return np.empty(0,float)
    la=min(len(a),m); lb=min(len(b),m)
    if la<=0 or lb<=0: return np.zeros(m,float)
    if la*lb <= 2048:
       r=np.convolve(a[:la], b[:lb])
       if len(r) >= m: return r[:m].astype(float, copy=False)
       out=np.zeros(m,float); out[:len(r)]=r; return out
    out_len=la+lb-1
    N=_next_pow2(out_len)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a[:la], N)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(b[:lb], N)
    r=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb, N)
    if len(r) >= m: return r[:m]
    out=np.zeros(m,float); out[:len(r)]=r; return out
```
If out_len < m, N based on out_len, r shorter, pad. Good.

Poly inv:
```
def _poly_inv(c,m):
    c0=float(c[0])
    if not math.isfinite(c0) or abs(c0) <= 1e-300: return None
    B=np.array([1.0/c0],float)
    l=1
    while l < m:
       nl=min(2*l,m)
       CB=_poly_mul_trunc(c[:nl], B, nl)
       factor = -CB
       factor[0] += 2.0
       B=_poly_mul_trunc(B, factor, nl)
       l=nl
    return B
```
Potential: If c is A and length l shorter than nl, c[:nl] returns length l; `_poly_mul_trunc` uses la=min(len(c[:nl]),nl)=len(c)=l. Good. But factor length nl may have B multiplier beyond? okay.

Sqrt newton check:
```
def _sqrt_newton(c,m):
    c=np.asarray(c,float)
    if c.size < m:
       tmp=np.zeros(m,float); tmp[:c.size]=c; c=tmp
    c0=float(c[0])
    if not math.isfinite(c0) or c0 <= 1e-300: return None
    A=np.array([math.sqrt(c0)],float)
    l=1
    while l < m:
       nl=min(2*l,m)
       B=_poly_inv(A,nl)
       if B is None: return None
       CB=_poly_mul_trunc(c[:nl], B, nl)
       Apad=np.zeros(nl,float)
       Apad[:l]=A
       A=0.5*(Apad+CB)
       l=nl
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(A)): return None
    # quick check
    chk=min(m, 32)
    a0=float(A[0])
    for k in range(1, chk):
       if k>1:
          s=float(np.dot(A[1:k], A[k-1:0:-1]))
       else: s=0.0
       val=2.0*a0*A[k]+s
       if not math.isfinite(val): return None
       ref=float(c[k])
       tol=1e-6*(1.0+abs(ref)+abs(val))
       if abs(val-ref) > tol:
           return None
    return A
```
Tolerance 1e-6 maybe okay; Newton roundoff could be ~1e-12 relative for small, but for c ~1e-4, 1e-6*1 ~1e-6 too loose? Actually 1e-6 absolute, too loose but okay. If bug large, fail. Could set 1e-7.

`_cspace_sqrt`:
```
def _cspace_sqrt(c,n):
    c=np.asarray(c,float)
    if c.size < n:
       tmp=np.zeros(n,float); tmp[:c.size]=c; c=tmp
    if c[0] <= 1e-300: return None
    A=None
    if n <= 128:
       A=_sqrt_direct(c,n)
    else:
       try: A=_sqrt_newton(c,n)
       except Exception: A=None
       if A is None and n <= 4096:
          try: A=_sqrt_direct(c,n)
          except: A=None
    if A is None: return None
    A=np.where(np.isfinite(A), A, 0.0)
    return A
```
If Newton fails at n>4096, no fallback. Could direct for n<=8192? Maybe add 8192 fallback if time. Let's set fallback n<=8192. Direct 33M ops maybe okay as rare. Use 6000? We'll set 6000 to avoid too slow. Actually if Newton fails rare, direct 8192 maybe okay. Set 8192.

C-space iter:
```
def _cspace_iter(C, M, blend):
    C=np.asarray(C,float)
    if C.size < 3 or not np.all(np.isfinite(C)): return None,None,np.inf
    n=(C.size+1)//2
    if n <= 1: return _normalize(C[:1]), C, float(C.max())
    Cp=_project_box_sum(C,M)
    A=_cspace_sqrt(Cp,n)
    if A is None: return None,None,np.inf
    A=np.where(np.isfinite(A),A,0.0)
    neg_sum=float(-np.sum(A[A<0.0])) if np.any(A<0.0) else 0.0
    pos_sum=float(np.sum(A[A>0.0]))
    if pos_sum <= 1e-300 or neg_sum > 0.25*pos_sum:
        # maybe clip anyway? If fail, try clip but only if small
        if neg_sum > 0.6*pos_sum:
            return None,None,np.inf
    A=np.maximum(A,0.0)
    s=float(A.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15: return None,None,np.inf
    A=A/s
    Cn=_auto_conv(A)
    mn=float(Cn.max())
    if not math.isfinite(mn): return A,Cn,mn
    Cnext=(1.0-blend)*Cn + blend*Cp
    Cnext=np.where(np.isfinite(Cnext), Cnext, 0.0)
    Cnext=np.maximum(Cnext,0.0)
    ss=float(Cnext.sum())
    if ss <= 1e-15:
       Cnext=Cp.copy()
    else:
       Cnext=Cnext/ss
    return A,Cnext,mn
```
If neg_sum >0.25 pos but <=0.6, proceed with clipping. Good.

`_cspace_feasible`: as above. Need handle `M` maybe nan.
```
def _cspace_feasible(a, M, max_iter, blend, deadline):
    a=_normalize(a)
    n=len(a)
    C=_auto_conv(a)
    m_cur=float(C.max())
    best_a=a.copy(); best_m=m_cur
    if m_cur <= M*(1+1e-9)+1e-15: return a
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       A,Cn,mnew=_cspace_iter(C,M,blend)
       if A is None: break
       if isinstance? no
       if mnew < best_m:
          best_a=A.copy(); best_m=mnew
       if mnew <= M*(1+1e-9)+1e-15:
          return A
       if Cn is not None:
          if np.max(np.abs(Cn-C)) < 1e-13*max(1.0,M):
             C=Cn
             break
          else:
             C=Cn
       else: break
    return best_a
```
`mnew` from `_cspace_iter` is Cn max before Cnext? It returns Cn. Good.

`_cspace_descent`:
```
def _cspace_descent(a, deadline, max_iter_large=8, max_iter_small=22, blend=0.1):
    a=_normalize(a)
    n=len(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=float('inf')
    sc_cur=2*n*float(_auto_conv(a).max())
    best_sc=sc_cur
    iters = max_iter_large if n > 8192 else max_iter_small
    delta=0.004
    stall=0
    lower_m=1.0/(2*n-1)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       if delta < 1e-5: break
       C=_auto_conv(a); m_cur=float(C.max())
       Mtarget=m_cur*(1.0-delta)
       if Mtarget < lower_m*1.0001: Mtarget=lower_m*1.0001
       A=_cspace_feasible(a, Mtarget, iters, blend, deadline-2)
       scA=2*n*float(_auto_conv(A).max())
       if scA < best_sc - 1e-14:
          best_a=A.copy(); best_sc=scA
          print(f"  cspace: score={scA:.10f}")
       if scA < sc_cur - 1e-14:
          a=A.copy(); sc_cur=scA; stall=0
          delta=min(0.03, delta*1.2)
       else:
          stall +=1
          delta *= 0.55
          if stall > 6 and delta < 1e-4: break
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
    return best_a, best_sc
```
This recomputes `_auto_conv(A)`; feasible could return best_a maybe same. Good. But `a` not normalized after `_cspace_feasible`? It returns normalized best. Good.

Shape search:
```
def _cspace_shape_search(n, specs, deadline):
    best=None; best_sc=np.inf
    for kind,p in specs:
       a=_profile(n,kind,p)
       sc=_score(a)
       if sc < best_sc: best=a; best_sc=sc
    if best is None: return None, np.inf
    # quick descent
   td = time.monotonic() + 60.0
    deadline_desc = min(deadline - 20.0, td)
    if deadline_desc > time.monotonic() + 5:
       a_d, sc_d = _cspace_descent(best, deadline_desc, max_iter_large=12, max_iter_small=25, blend=0.12)
       if sc_d < best_sc: best, best_sc = a_d, sc_d
    low=max(1.0001, best_sc - 0.10)
    high=best_sc
    max_iter = 18 if n <= 4096 else 10
    for i in range(14):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
       if high - low < 1e-5: break
       mid=(low+high)*0.5
       M=mid/(2.0*n)
       A=_cspace_feasible(best, M, max_iter, 0.18, deadline-3)
       scA=_score(A)
       if np.isfinite(scA):
          if scA <= mid*(1+1e-7):
             high=mid; best=A.copy(); best_sc=scA
          else:
             low=mid
             if scA < best_sc:
                best=A.copy(); best_sc=scA
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
    return best,best_sc
```
If best_sc inf? no.

`_run_lbfgs`:
```
def _run_lbfgs(a_init, deadline, max_beta_count=16):
    a=_normalize(a_init)
    n=len(a)
    sc_cur=_score(a)
    if not finite: return a
    z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z -= z.mean()
    c=_auto_conv(a); m=float(c.max())
    if m <=0: return a
    gap = m
    if len(c) > 100:
       c_sorted=np.sort(c)[::-1]
       gap = c_sorted[0] - c_sorted[min(50,len(c)-1)]
    if gap < m*0.0005: gap=m*0.0005
    base_beta=max(gap, m*0.003, 1e-12)
    ratios=np.array([1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03,0.015,0.008,0.004,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002,0.0001,0.00005,0.00002])
    for ratio in ratios[:max_beta_count]:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
       beta=base_beta*ratio
       if beta <= 0: break
       remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
       scale=max(1.0, n/8192.0)
       maxiter=int(min(1200, max(30, remaining/(0.05*scale+1e-9))))
       if maxiter < 10: break
       try:
          res=minimize(_lse_fun, z, args=(beta,n), jac=_lse_jac, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-11,'maxcor':60,'maxls':25,'disp':False})
          a_new=_softmax(res.x)
          sc_new=_score(a_new)
          if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < sc_cur - 1e-15:
             a=a_new; sc_cur=sc_new
             z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); z-=z.mean()
             # optionally recompute base? not
       except Exception:
          pass
    return a
```
`_score` recomputes; okay. `res.x` may have large values; softmax.

`_active_polish` code. Need be careful time and top_k.
```
def _active_polish(a, deadline, tol_start=0.002, tol_end=1e-6, max_line_eval=10):
    a=_normalize(a)
    n=len(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=2*n*float(_auto_conv(a).max())
    tol=tol_start; no_improve=0; stall=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1:
       c=_auto_conv(a); m=float(c.max()); sc=2*n*m
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
          best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
          # compute direction; if already best, continue
       Nc=2*n-1
       beta_act=max(tol*m, m*1e-6)
       active = c >= m - max(tol,1e-9)*m
       n_act=int(active.sum())
       top_k = max(10, min(250, n//32))
       if n_act == 0:
          active=np.zeros(Nc,bool); active[int(np.argmax(c))]=True; n_act=1
       elif n_act < top_k:
          k=min(top_k,Nc)
          if k < Nc:
             idx=np.argpartition(c, Nc-k)[Nc-k:]
          else:
             idx=np.arange(Nc)
          active=np.zeros(Nc,bool); active[idx]=True
       elif n_act > max(5*top_k, 500):
          k=top_k
          idx=np.argpartition(c, Nc-k)[Nc-k:]
          active=np.zeros(Nc,bool); active[idx]=True
       vals=(c[active]-m)/beta_act
       ew=np.exp(np.clip(vals,-700.0,0.0))
       s_ew=float(ew.sum())
       w=np.zeros(Nc,float)
       if s_ew > 1e-300:
          w[active]=ew/s_ew
       else:
          w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
       ar=a[::-1]
       wc=_convolve_fast(w, ar)
       h=wc[n-1:2*n-1]
       grad=2.0*h
       grad_c=grad - np.mean(grad)
       d=-grad_c
       dn=float(np.linalg.norm(d))
       if dn < 1e-18:
          break
       neg=d < -1e-300
       if np.any(neg):
          alpha_max=float(np.min(a[neg]/(-d[neg])))
       else:
          alpha_max=np.inf
       alpha_max=min(alpha_max*0.99, 0.5/(dn+1e-15), 0.5)
       if alpha_max <= 1e-15:
          no_improve +=1
          if no_improve % 20 ==0:
             tol=max(tol_end, tol*0.5)
          if no_improve >= 100: break
          continue
       sc_new, alpha, a_new = _line_search_sample(a,d,alpha_max,n,sc,max_line_eval)
       if sc_new < sc - 1e-15:
          a=a_new
          if tol > tol_end:
             tol=max(tol_end, tol*0.99)
          no_improve=0
       else:
          # try single top peak sometimes
          improve=False
          k_top=int(np.argmax(c))
          w2=np.zeros(Nc,float); w2[k_top]=1.0
          wc2=_convolve_fast(w2, ar)
          h2=wc2[n-1:2*n-1]
          d2=-(2.0*h2 - np.mean(2.0*h2))
          dn2=float(np.linalg.norm(d2))
          if dn2 > 1e-18:
             neg2=d2 < -1e-300
             if np.any(neg2): amax2=float(np.min(a[neg2]/(-d2[neg2])))
             else: amax2=np.inf
             amax2=min(amax2*0.99, 0.5/(dn2+1e-15), 0.5)
             if amax2 > 1e-15:
                sc2,_,a2=_line_search_sample(a,d2,amax2,n,sc,max_line_eval)
                if sc2 < sc - 1e-15:
                   a=a2; no_improve=0; improve=True
          if not improve:
             no_improve +=1
             if no_improve % 25 ==0:
                tol=max(tol_end, tol*0.55)
             if no_improve >= 120:
                a=best_a.copy(); no_improve=0; stall +=1
                if stall > 12: break
    return best_a,best_sc
```
`_line_search_sample` uses f0=sc. If sc_new at alpha=0 equals sc; returns a unchanged if no improvement. Need if `alpha=0`, a_new=a.

`_line_search_sample` implementation earlier. Use `math`? no.

Potential issue: In active, after no_improve reset due top-peak improve, `best_sc` not updated until next loop; fine.

Time: If active loop with line search 10 evals and top-peak extra 10 when stall, could be heavy. deadline controls.

def _make_candidates(base_a, max_n=24576):
   cands=[]
   n_base=len(base_a)
   ns=[n_base]
   for x in (16384,20480,24576):

   for n in sorted(set(ns)):
      arrs=[]

Final validation and return:
```
    out=np.asarray(best_a,float)
    out=np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out=np.clip(out,0.0,1000.0)
    s=out.sum()

```

def _line_search_sample(a, d, alpha_max, n, f0, max_eval=12):
    best_sc=f0
    best_alpha=0.0
    if alpha_max <= 1e-15 or not math.isfinite(f0): return best_sc,best_alpha,a.copy()
    fracs=(0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.7,0.9,0.96,0.99)
    for frac in fracs:
       alpha=frac*alpha_max
       at=a+alpha*d
       if np.any(at < 0.0):
          at=np.maximum(at,0.0)
       s=float(at.sum())
       if s <= 1e-15: continue
       at=at/s
       c=_auto_conv(at)
       sc=2.0*n*float(c.max())
       if sc < best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best_alpha=alpha
    if best_alpha > 0.0:
       at=a+best_alpha*d
       at=np.maximum(at,0.0)
       s=at.sum()
       if s>0: at=at/s
       return best_sc,best_alpha,at
    return best_sc,best_alpha,a.copy()

def _project_box_sum(c, M):
    c=np.asarray(c,float)
    c=np.where(np.isfinite(c),c,0.0)
    s=float(c.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
       return np.ones_like(c)/len(c)
    c=c/s
    Nc=len(c)
    if M <= 0:
       return c
    if M >= 1.0: # since sum1, max always <=1, no effect
       return c
    avg=1.0/Nc

       # infeasible, return flat?

    x=np.minimum(c, M)
    deficit=1.0 - float(x.sum())

       x=np.maximum(x,0.0)
       ss=float(x.sum())
       if ss > 0: x=x/ss
       return x

    sl=float(slack.sum())

             # if final scale changes max maybe ok if small
             x=x/ss
             x=np.clip(x,0.0,M)

          x=x/float(x.sum())

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

The program combines several complementary approaches:
- C-space alternating projections: cap the square-coefficients conv(a,a)
  at a target maximum, redistribute mass, take a formal polynomial
  square root, normalize, and iterate.
- Log-domain LSE L-BFGS optimization of a smooth surrogate.
- Active-set peak shaving with sampled line searches.
- Multi-length resampling / profile starts.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def _next_pow2(n):
    if n <= 1:
        return 1
    k = 1
    while k < n:
        k <<= 1
    return k


def _convolve_fast(a, b):
    out_n = len(a) + len(b) - 1
    if out_n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    N = _next_pow2(out_n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, N)[:out_n]


def _auto_conv(a):
    n = len(a)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    N = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:2 * n - 1]


def _score(a):
    if a is None:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return float("inf")
    c = _auto_conv(a)
    m = float(c.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * len(a) * m / (s * s))


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=float)
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones_like(a, dtype=float) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def _profile(n, kind="power", p=0.5, alpha=0.0, freq=2, amp=0.3, phase=0.0):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=float)
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    tt = t / float(max(n - 1, 1))
    if kind == "uniform":
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    elif kind == "power":
        a = (t + 1.0 + alpha) ** (-float(p))
    elif kind == "power_rev":
        a = (n - t) ** (-float(p))
    elif kind == "exp":
        a = np.exp(-3.0 * t / float(max(n, 1)))
    elif kind == "cos":
        a = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * float(freq) * tt + phase)
    else:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        a = a / s
    return a


def _resample(a, new_n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    old_n = len(a)
    if old_n == new_n:
        return _normalize(a)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    b = np.interp(x1, x0, a)
    return _normalize(b)


def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -700.0, 700.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(z, dtype=float) / float(z.size)
    return e / s


def _lse_fun(z, beta, n):
    a = _softmax(z)
    c = _auto_conv(a)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    shifted = (c - cmax) / beta
    e = np.exp(np.clip(shifted, -700.0, 0.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return 2.0 * n * cmax
    return float(2.0 * n * (cmax + beta * math.log(s)))


def _lse_jac(z, beta, n):
    a = _softmax(z)
    c = _auto_conv(a)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    shifted = (c - cmax) / beta
    e = np.exp(np.clip(shifted, -700.0, 0.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=float)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = e / s
    W = float(np.dot(w, c))
    ar = a[::-1]
    wc = _convolve_fast(w, ar)
    h = wc[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    return 4.0 * n * a * (h - W)


def _run_lbfgs(a_init, deadline, max_beta_count=16):
    a = _normalize(a_init)
    n = len(a)
    sc_cur = _score(a)
    if not math.isfinite(sc_cur):
        return a

    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    z = z - z.mean()

    c = _auto_conv(a)
    m = float(c.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        return a

    gap = m
    if len(c) > 100:
        c_sorted = np.sort(c)[::-1]
        gap = c_sorted[0] - c_sorted[min(50, len(c) - 1)]
    if gap < m * 0.0005:
        gap = m * 0.0005
    base_beta = max(gap, m * 0.003, 1e-12)

    ratios = np.array([
        1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03, 0.015, 0.008,
        0.004, 0.002, 0.001, 0.0005, 0.0002, 0.0001, 0.00005, 0.00002
    ])

    for ratio in ratios[:max_beta_count]:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
            break
        beta = base_beta * float(ratio)
        if beta <= 0.0:
            break
        remaining = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
        scale = max(1.0, n / 8192.0)
        maxiter = int(min(1200, max(30, remaining / (0.05 * scale + 1e-9))))
        if maxiter < 10:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _lse_fun, z, args=(beta, n), jac=_lse_jac,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "ftol": 1e-15,
                    "gtol": 1e-11,
                    "maxcor": 60,
                    "maxls": 25,
                    "disp": False,
                }
            )
            a_new = _softmax(res.x)
            sc_new = _score(a_new)
            if math.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < sc_cur - 1e-15:
                a = a_new
                sc_cur = sc_new
                z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
                z = z - z.mean()
        except Exception:
            pass
    return a


def _project_box_sum(c, M):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    s = float(c.sum())
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones_like(c, dtype=float) / float(c.size)
    c = c / s
    Nc = len(c)
    if M <= 0.0:
        return c
    if M >= 1.0:
        return c
    avg = 1.0 / float(Nc)
    if M < avg * 0.999:
        return np.full(Nc, avg, dtype=float)

    x = np.minimum(c, M)
    deficit = 1.0 - float(x.sum())
    if deficit <= 1e-14:
        x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
        ss = float(x.sum())
        if ss > 0.0:
            x = x / ss
        return x

    mask = x < M
    if not np.any(mask):
        ss = float(x.sum())
        return x / ss if ss > 0.0 else c

    slack = M - x[mask]
    sl = float(slack.sum())
    if sl <= 1e-300:
        return x

    x = x.copy()
    x[mask] += deficit * (slack / sl)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, M)
    ss = float(x.sum())
    if ss <= 0.0:
        return c

    diff = 1.0 - ss
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        free = x < (M - 1e-14)
        nf = int(np.count_nonzero(free))
        if nf > 0:
            x[free] += diff / float(nf)
            x = np.clip(x, 0.0, M)
            ss = float(x.sum())
            if ss > 0.0:
                x = x / ss
                x = np.clip(x, 0.0, M)
        else:
            x = x / float(x.sum())
    return x


def _sqrt_direct(c, n):
    c0 = float(c[0])
    if not math.isfinite(c0) or c0 <= 1e-300:
        return None
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    a0 = math.sqrt(c0)
    a[0] = a0
    inv = 1.0 / (2.0 * a0)
    for k in range(1, n):
        if k > 1:
            s = float(np.dot(a[1:k], a[k - 1:0:-1]))
        else:
            s = 0.0
        if not math.isfinite(s):
            return None
        val = (float(c[k]) - s) * inv
        if not math.isfinite(val):
            return None
        a[k] = val
    return a


def _poly_mul_trunc(a, b, m):
    if m <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    la = min(len(a), m)
    lb = min(len(b), m)
    if la <= 0 or lb <= 0:
        return np.zeros(m, dtype=float)

    if la * lb <= 2048:
        r = np.convolve(a[:la], b[:lb])
        if len(r) >= m:
            return r[:m].astype(float, copy=False)
        out = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
        out[:len(r)] = r
        return out

    out_len = la + lb - 1
    N = _next_pow2(out_len)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a[:la], N)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b[:lb], N)
    r = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, N)
    if len(r) >= m:
        return r[:m]
    out = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
    out[:len(r)] = r
    return out


def _poly_inv(c, m):
    c0 = float(c[0])
    if not math.isfinite(c0) or abs(c0) <= 1e-300:
        return None
    B = np.array([1.0 / c0], dtype=float)
    l = 1
    while l < m:
        nl = min(2 * l, m)
        CB = _poly_mul_trunc(c[:nl], B, nl)
        if CB is None:
            return None
        factor = -CB
        factor[0] += 2.0
        B = _poly_mul_trunc(B, factor, nl)
        if B is None:
            return None
        l = nl
    return B


def _sqrt_newton(c, m):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    if c.size < m:
        tmp = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
        tmp[:c.size] = c
        c = tmp

    c0 = float(c[0])
    if not math.isfinite(c0) or c0 <= 1e-300:
        return None

    A = np.array([math.sqrt(c0)], dtype=float)
    l = 1
    while l < m:
        nl = min(2 * l, m)
        B = _poly_inv(A, nl)
        if B is None:
            return None
        CB = _poly_mul_trunc(c[:nl], B, nl)
        if CB is None:
            return None
        Apad = np.zeros(nl, dtype=float)
        Apad[:l] = A
        A = 0.5 * (Apad + CB)
        l = nl

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(A)):
        return None

    chk = min(m, 32)
    a0 = float(A[0])
    for k in range(1, chk):
        if k > 1:
            s = float(np.dot(A[1:k], A[k - 1:0:-1]))
        else:
            s = 0.0
        if not math.isfinite(s):
            return None
        val = 2.0 * a0 * A[k] + s
        ref = float(c[k])
        if not math.isfinite(val) or not math.isfinite(ref):
            return None
        tol = 1e-6 * (1.0 + abs(ref) + abs(val))
        if abs(val - ref) > tol:
            return None
    return A


def _cspace_sqrt(c, n):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    if c.size < n:
        tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        tmp[:c.size] = c
        c = tmp
    if n <= 1:
        c0 = float(c[0])
        if c0 <= 0.0:
            return None
        return np.array([math.sqrt(c0)], dtype=float)

    if float(c[0]) <= 1e-300:
        return None

    A = None
    if n <= 128:
        A = _sqrt_direct(c, n)
    else:
        try:
            A = _sqrt_newton(c, n)
        except Exception:
            A = None
        if A is None and n <= 8192:
            try:
                A = _sqrt_direct(c, n)
            except Exception:
                A = None

    if A is None:
        return None
    A = np.where(np.isfinite(A), A, 0.0)
    return A


def _cspace_iter(C, M, blend):
    C = np.asarray(C, dtype=float)
    if C.size < 3 or not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
        return None, None, float("inf")
    n = (C.size + 1) // 2
    if n <= 1:
        a = _normalize(C[:1])
        return a, C.copy(), float(C.max())

    Cp = _project_box_sum(C, M)
    A = _cspace_sqrt(Cp, n)
    if A is None:
        return None, None, float("inf")

    A = np.where(np.isfinite(A), A, 0.0)
    neg_sum = float(-np.sum(A[A < 0.0])) if np.any(A < 0.0) else 0.0
    pos_sum = float(np.sum(A[A > 0.0]))
    if pos_sum <= 1e-300:
        return None, None, float("inf")
    if neg_sum > 0.6 * pos_sum:
        return None, None, float("inf")

    A = np.maximum(A, 0.0)
    s = float(A.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return None, None, float("inf")
    A = A / s

    Cn = _auto_conv(A)
    mn = float(Cn.max())
    if not math.isfinite(mn):
        return A, Cn, float("inf")

    Cnext = (1.0 - blend) * Cn + blend * Cp
    Cnext = np.where(np.isfinite(Cnext), Cnext, 0.0)
    Cnext = np.maximum(Cnext, 0.0)
    ss = float(Cnext.sum())
    if ss <= 1e-15:
        Cnext = Cp.copy()
    else:
        Cnext = Cnext / ss
    return A, Cnext, mn


def _cspace_feasible(a, M, max_iter, blend, deadline):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = len(a)
    C = _auto_conv(a)
    m_cur = float(C.max())
    if not math.isfinite(m_cur):
        return a

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_m = m_cur
    if m_cur <= M * (1.0 + 1e-9) + 1e-15:
        return a

    for _ in range(max_iter):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        A, Cn, mnew = _cspace_iter(C, M, blend)
        if A is None:
            break
        if math.isfinite(mnew) and mnew < best_m:
            best_a = A.copy()
            best_m = mnew
        if math.isfinite(mnew) and mnew <= M * (1.0 + 1e-9) + 1e-15:
            return A
        if Cn is not None:
            if np.max(np.abs(Cn - C)) < 1e-13 * max(1.0, M):
                C = Cn
                break
            else:
                C = Cn
        else:
            break
    return best_a


def _cspace_descent(a, deadline, max_iter_large=8, max_iter_small=22, blend=0.1):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = len(a)
    C = _auto_conv(a)
    m_cur = float(C.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = 2.0 * n * m_cur
    sc_cur = best_sc

    iters = max_iter_large if n > 8192 else max_iter_small
    delta = 0.004
    stall = 0
    lower_m = 1.0 / float(2 * n - 1)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if delta < 1e-5:
            break

        C = _auto_conv(a)
        m_cur = float(C.max())
        if not math.isfinite(m_cur):
            break
        Mtarget = m_cur * (1.0 - delta)
        if Mtarget < lower_m * 1.0001:
            Mtarget = lower_m * 1.0001

        A = _cspace_feasible(a, Mtarget, iters, blend, max(deadline - 2.0, time.monotonic()))
        scA = _score(A)
        if not math.isfinite(scA):
            break

        if scA < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best_a = A.copy()
            best_sc = scA

        if scA < sc_cur - 1e-14:
            a = A.copy()
            sc_cur = scA
            stall = 0
            delta = min(0.03, delta * 1.2)
        else:
            stall += 1
            delta *= 0.55
            if stall > 8 and delta < 1e-4:
                break

        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
            break
    return best_a, best_sc


def _cspace_shape_search(n, specs, deadline):
    best = None
    best_sc = float("inf")

    for kind, p in specs:
        a = _profile(n, kind, p)
        sc = _score(a)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best = a
            best_sc = sc

    if best is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    deadline_desc = min(deadline - 20.0, time.monotonic() + 60.0)
    if deadline_desc > time.monotonic() + 5.0:
        try:
            a_d, sc_d = _cspace_descent(
                best, deadline_desc,
                max_iter_large=12, max_iter_small=25, blend=0.12
            )
            if math.isfinite(sc_d) and sc_d < best_sc:
                best, best_sc = a_d, sc_d
        except Exception:
            pass

    low = max(1.0001, best_sc - 0.10)
    high = best_sc
    max_iter = 18 if n <= 4096 else 10

    for _ in range(14):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
            break
        if high - low < 1e-5:
            break
        mid = 0.5 * (low + high)
        M = mid / (2.0 * float(n))
        try:
            A = _cspace_feasible(best, M, max_iter, 0.18, max(deadline - 3.0, time.monotonic()))
        except Exception:
            break
        scA = _score(A)
        if not math.isfinite(scA):
            break
        if scA <= mid * (1.0 + 1e-7):
            high = mid
            best = A.copy()
            best_sc = scA
        else:
            low = mid
            if scA < best_sc:
                best = A.copy()
                best_sc = scA

    return best, best_sc


def _line_search_sample(a, d, alpha_max, n, f0, max_eval=12):
    best_sc = f0
    best_alpha = 0.0
    if alpha_max <= 1e-15 or not math.isfinite(f0):
        return best_sc, best_alpha, a.copy()

    fracs = (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.50, 0.70, 0.90, 0.96, 0.99)
    for frac in fracs[:max_eval]:
        alpha = frac * alpha_max
        at = a + alpha * d
        at = np.maximum(at, 0.0)
        s = float(at.sum())
        if s <= 1e-15:
            continue
        at = at / s
        c = _auto_conv(at)
        sc = 2.0 * n * float(c.max())
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_alpha = alpha

    if best_alpha > 0.0:
        at = a + best_alpha * d
        at = np.maximum(at, 0.0)
        s = float(at.sum())
        if s > 1e-15:
            at = at / s
            return best_sc, best_alpha, at
    return best_sc, best_alpha, a.copy()


def _active_polish(a, deadline, tol_start=0.002, tol_end=1e-6, max_line_eval=12):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = len(a)
    c = _auto_conv(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = 2.0 * n * float(c.max())

    tol = tol_start
    no_improve = 0
    stall = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
        c = _auto_conv(a)
        m = float(c.max())
        sc = 2.0 * n * m
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0

        Nc = 2 * n - 1
        beta_act = max(tol * m, m * 1e-6)

        active = c >= (m - max(tol, 1e-9) * m)
        n_act = int(active.sum())
        top_k = max(10, min(250, n // 32))

        if n_act == 0:
            active = np.zeros(Nc, dtype=bool)
            active[int(np.argmax(c))] = True
            n_act = 1
        elif n_act < top_k:
            k = min(top_k, Nc)
            if k < Nc:
                idx = np.argpartition(c, Nc - k)[Nc - k:]
            else:
                idx = np.arange(Nc)
            active = np.zeros(Nc, dtype=bool)
            active[idx] = True
        elif n_act > max(5 * top_k, 500):
            k = top_k
            idx = np.argpartition(c, Nc - k)[Nc - k:]
            active = np.zeros(Nc, dtype=bool)
            active[idx] = True

        vals = (c[active] - m) / beta_act
        ew = np.exp(np.clip(vals, -700.0, 0.0))
        s_ew = float(ew.sum())
        w = np.zeros(Nc, dtype=float)
        if s_ew > 1e-300:
            w[active] = ew / s_ew
        else:
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0

        ar = a[::-1]
        wc = _convolve_fast(w, ar)
        h = wc[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
        grad = 2.0 * h
        grad_c = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
        d = -grad_c
        dn = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
        if dn < 1e-18:
            break

        neg = d < -1e-300
        if np.any(neg):
            alpha_max = float(np.min(a[neg] / (-d[neg])))
        else:
            alpha_max = float("inf")
        alpha_max = min(alpha_max * 0.99, 0.5 / (dn + 1e-15), 0.5)

        if alpha_max <= 1e-15:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve % 25 == 0:
                tol = max(tol_end, tol * 0.5)
            if no_improve >= 120:
                break
            continue

        sc_new, alpha, a_new = _line_search_sample(a, d, alpha_max, n, sc, max_line_eval)
        if math.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < sc - 1e-15:
            a = a_new
            if tol > tol_end:
                tol = max(tol_end, tol * 0.99)
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            improved = False
            k_top = int(np.argmax(c))
            try:
                w2 = np.zeros(Nc, dtype=float)
                w2[k_top] = 1.0
                wc2 = _convolve_fast(w2, ar)
                h2 = wc2[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
                d2 = -(2.0 * h2 - float(np.mean(2.0 * h2)))
                dn2 = float(np.linalg.norm(d2))
                if dn2 > 1e-18:
                    neg2 = d2 < -1e-300
                    if np.any(neg2):
                        amax2 = float(np.min(a[neg2] / (-d2[neg2])))
                    else:
                        amax2 = float("inf")
                    amax2 = min(amax2 * 0.99, 0.5 / (dn2 + 1e-15), 0.5)
                    if amax2 > 1e-15:
                        sc2, _, a2 = _line_search_sample(a, d2, amax2, n, sc, max_line_eval)
                        if math.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < sc - 1e-15:
                            a = a2
                            no_improve = 0
                            improved = True
            except Exception:
                pass

            if not improved:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve % 25 == 0:
                    tol = max(tol_end, tol * 0.55)
                if no_improve >= 120:
                    a = best_a.copy()
                    no_improve = 0
                    stall += 1
                    if stall > 12:
                        break

    return best_a, best_sc


def _top_candidates(base_a, max_cands=3, max_n=20480):
    base_a = np.asarray(base_a, dtype=float)
    n_base = len(base_a)
    ns = [n_base, 16384, 20480, 24576]
    seen = set()
    cands = []

    for n in ns:
        if n <= 0 or n > max_n or n in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(n)
        arrs = []
        if n == n_base:
            arrs.append(base_a.copy())
        else:
            arrs.append(_resample(base_a, n))
            arrs.append(_profile(n, "power", 0.50))
            arrs.append(_profile(n, "power", 0.45))

        best_arr = None
        best_sc = float("inf")
        for arr in arrs:
            arr = _normalize(arr)
            sc = _score(arr)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_arr = arr
                best_sc = sc
        if best_arr is not None:
            cands.append([best_sc, n, best_arr])

    top = []
    while len(top) < max_cands and cands:
        bi = 0
        for i in range(1, len(cands)):
            if cands[i][0] < cands[bi][0]:
                bi = i
        top.append(cands.pop(bi))
    return top


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800.0):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 20.0)

    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float).ravel()
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if p.size > 0 and s > 0.01:
            parent = p / s
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None:
        parent = _profile(8192, "power", 0.50)

    best_a = parent.copy()
    best_sc = _score(best_a)
    n_best = len(best_a)
    print(f"init n={n_best} score={best_sc:.10f}", flush=True)

    specs = [
        ("power", 0.35),
        ("power", 0.42),
        ("power", 0.50),
        ("power", 0.55),
        ("power", 0.65),
        ("power_rev", 0.50),
        ("uniform", 0.0),
        ("exp", 0.0),
        ("cos", 0.0),
    ]

    for kind, p in specs:
        if time.monotonic() > t0 + 30.0:
            break
        try:
            a = _profile(n_best, kind, p)
            sc = _score(a)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_a = a
                best_sc = sc
                n_best = len(a)
                print(f"profile {kind}/{p}: score={best_sc:.10f}", flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if len(best_a) <= 24576:
        dlC = min(t0 + 350.0, deadline - 700.0)
        if dlC > time.monotonic() + 30.0:
            try:
                a_c, sc_c = _cspace_descent(
                    best_a, dlC,
                    max_iter_large=8, max_iter_small=22, blend=0.10
                )
                if math.isfinite(sc_c) and sc_c < best_sc:
                    best_a = a_c
                    best_sc = sc_c
                    n_best = len(a_c)
                    print(f"cspace large: score={best_sc:.10f}", flush=True)
            except Exception:
                pass

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 600.0:
        n_shape = 2048 if len(best_a) < 16384 else 4096
        shape_specs = [
            ("power", 0.35),
            ("power", 0.45),
            ("power", 0.50),
            ("power", 0.55),
            ("power", 0.65),
            ("uniform", 0.0),
            ("exp", 0.0),
        ]
        dlS = min(t0 + 650.0, deadline - 600.0)
        if dlS > time.monotonic() + 30.0:
            try:
                a_s, sc_s = _cspace_shape_search(n_shape, shape_specs, dlS)
                if a_s is not None and math.isfinite(sc_s):
                    targets = {len(best_a), 16384, 20480}
                    for target_n in sorted(targets):
                        if target_n <= 0 or target_n > 20480:
                            continue
                        if time.monotonic() > dlS - 5.0:
                            break
                        a_r = _resample(a_s, target_n)
                        sc_r = _score(a_r)
                        if math.isfinite(sc_r) and sc_r < best_sc:
                            best_a = a_r
                            best_sc = sc_r
                            n_best = target_n
                            print(f"shape n={target_n}: score={best_sc:.10f}", flush=True)
            except Exception:
                pass

    if len(best_a) <= 16384 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 400.0:
        try:
            sc_cur = best_sc
            low = max(1.0001, sc_cur - 0.05)
            high = sc_cur
            for _ in range(6):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30.0:
                    break
                mid = 0.5 * (low + high)
                M = mid / (2.0 * float(n_best))
                A = _cspace_feasible(best_a, M, 8, 0.15, max(deadline - 10.0, time.monotonic()))
                scA = _score(A)
                if not math.isfinite(scA):
                    break
                if scA <= mid * (1.0 + 1e-7):
                    high = mid
                    best_a = A
                    best_sc = scA
                else:
                    low = mid
                    if scA < best_sc:
                        best_a = A
                        best_sc = scA
                if high - low < 1e-5:
                    break
        except Exception:
            pass

    candidates = _top_candidates(best_a, 3, max_n=20480)
    if not candidates:
        candidates = [[best_sc, len(best_a), best_a.copy()]]

    for idx, item in enumerate(candidates):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 250.0:
            break
        sc_c, n_c, a_c = item
        if idx > 0 and math.isfinite(best_sc) and sc_c > best_sc + 0.01:
            continue
        dl_c = min(deadline - 250.0, time.monotonic() + 180.0)
        if dl_c <= time.monotonic() + 10.0:
            continue
        try:
            a_o = _run_lbfgs(a_c, dl_c, max_beta_count=16)
            sc_o = _score(a_o)
            if math.isfinite(sc_o) and sc_o < best_sc:
                best_a = a_o
                best_sc = sc_o
                n_best = len(a_o)
                print(f"lbfgs candidate n={n_best}: score={best_sc:.10f}", flush=True)

            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 250.0 and len(best_a) <= 16384:
                dl_cd = min(deadline - 250.0, time.monotonic() + 40.0)
                a_cd, sc_cd = _cspace_descent(
                    best_a, dl_cd,
                    max_iter_large=4, max_iter_small=10, blend=0.08
                )
                if math.isfinite(sc_cd) and sc_cd < best_sc:
                    best_a = a_cd
                    best_sc = sc_cd
                    n_best = len(a_best := best_a)
                    print(f"post lbfgs cspace n={n_best}: score={best_sc:.10f}", flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        try:
            a_f, sc_f = _active_polish(
                best_a, deadline - 2.0,
                tol_start=0.0015, tol_end=5e-7, max_line_eval=12
            )
            if math.isfinite(sc_f) and sc_f < best_sc:
                best_a = a_f
                best_sc = sc_f
                n_best = len(best_a)
        except Exception:
            pass

    out = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=float)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if out.size == 0 or s < 0.01:
        out = np.ones(1024, dtype=float) / 1024.0
    else:
        out = out / s

    final_score = _score(out)
    print(f"FINAL n={len(out)} score={final_score:.10f} time={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s", flush=True)
    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
